SUBJECT / t Joseph B. Smith - Recommendation for

: . Promotion from GS-14 to GS-15

! LL Mri Smith joined the Headquarters Training Branch of the
OTR Operations School in ‘October 1966 specifically to assume respon-
sibility for Covert Action training. “He spent the first few months in

a thorough study and review of all CGA courses previously given in OTR.
This was a long and complicated job, compounded “part by the public”
compromise of a wide range of Agency ‘CA activities, In closest: coordi+

_ nation with the GA Staff, Mr, Smith devised. a sotally Hew and different’.
le "CA curriculum, ‘consisting of twu short seminars for middlé and senior.
. gtade officers -- one called a Covert Actién Operations Seminar and

one a Politics Workshop, These seminars, em ropha sizing the integrated
nature of CS operations, have evoked a very positive response, con-
firming the soundness of Mr. Smith's creative efforts in- Fevamping

CA \ training in this fashion, . :

2. “As a corollary to the main duties cited above, Mr, Smith-has

“alsa successfully run, in conjunction with FE Division, a Psychological

Warfare Operations Course for military officers being assigned ta
MACV/SOG in Saigon. Finally, in the CA iicld, Mr, Smith has pro-
vided tutorials, as requested by various ares Mvisidns, ro-a riumber
of Staff and Contract Agents and to foreign Haisen representatives: in
such fields aa hlack radio, youth and student operations, veterans
organizations, and methods of counts ering Cammunist propaganda,

sibility ag Vradecratt Advisor and ‘Coordinator : for the ritioag
Support Course, a critical black. of instruction fer (primarily young
